# Daily video prompt pack — 24/10/2026 · Goat day (ថ្ងៃមមែ)

Lucky signs: Goat, Rabbit, Pig · Careful: Ox

## Luckiest animal today – Goat (មមែ) · Great Day
One hero post for the day's luckiest animal: a picture, a short video and a caption. Lucky color green (បៃតង) · best time 05:00–07:00 · lucky numbers 7 · 27 · 292 · 7772 · 27760 (for fun only).

### Picture prompt (square 1:1)
Create a premium-quality 3D animated fantasy character of an adorable golden goat with big soft eyes, curled shining horns and a tiny bell, the Goat of the Chinese zodiac, inspired by the charm and enchanting visual appeal of classic Disney and Pixar-style animated family films. The goat symbolizes peace, harmony and a caring heart: a gentle, kind and peaceful expression and pose that show this. It sits on a round golden pedestal inside a large glowing pink lotus floating on a still pond. Behind it, a huge glowing full moon with a soft silver-gold halo, a deep indigo starry sky, a softly illuminated Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat with warm golden lights, palm tree silhouettes, small floating lanterns, glowing lotus flowers on the water, gentle moonbeams falling on the goat, magical sparkles and soft silver-and-gold reflections. Cinematic lighting, soft atmospheric glow, realistic metallic reflections, beautiful depth of field, professional animated-movie-quality rendering. Square 1:1, full body visible, three-quarter view, centered, with space around the character. No text, no letters, no watermark, no logo, no cropped body parts.

## 60-second video – Facebook & TikTok
A 60-second video about the Goat, today's luckiest animal: 6 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add the reference picture of the Goat as an ingredient. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice": it copies the style line, the scene and the voice together. Put the clips in order 1 to 6.

Today's luckiest zodiac animal, the Goat, in one minute. For viewers who follow Chinese zodiac signs, especially people born in the Year of the Goat, scrolling Facebook Reels and TikTok.

### Opening hook – 5 options
1. Identity

   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមែមែនទេ? ថ្ងៃនេះ ជាថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំងសម្រាប់អ្នក។ មកមើលពណ៌ ពេលវេលា និងលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នក។
★ 2. Curiosity
   English: Out of all twelve zodiac signs, only one has a Great Day today. Is it yours?
   Khmer: ក្នុងចំណោមរាសីទាំង 12 មានតែមួយគត់ ដែលមានថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំងនៅថ្ងៃនេះ។ តើជារាសីរបស់អ្នកឬទេ?
3. Question
   English: Which zodiac animal is the luckiest today? Here's the answer, and what it means for you.
   Khmer: តើឆ្នាំណា មានសំណាងជាងគេនៅថ្ងៃនេះ? នេះគឺជាចម្លើយ និងអត្ថន័យសម្រាប់អ្នក។
4. Challenge
   English: Can you guess today's luckiest zodiac animal? Three clues: gentle, creative, and compassionate.
   Khmer: ទាយមើល៖ តើឆ្នាំណា មានសំណាងជាងគេថ្ងៃនេះ? តម្រុយ៖ សុភាពរាបសា ច្នៃប្រឌិត និងអាណិតអាសូរ។
5. Fact
   English: Today is a Goat day, and in the twelve-day zodiac cycle, that makes the Goat today's luckiest sign.
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃមមែ ហើយតាមវដ្ដដប់ពីរថ្ងៃ ឆ្នាំមមែ គឺជារាសីដែលមានសំណាងជាងគេថ្ងៃនេះ។
★ = used to open this video. Why: Specific and true (only the day's animal has a Great Day), creates curiosity, and every viewer wants to know if it is their sign.

### Audio design
Music: Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. Keep it well below the voice (about 20 dB quieter) so every word is clear. Sound effects, used lightly: a soft chime when a lucky fact is revealed, a gentle whoosh on scene changes, a warm swell at the ending. No loud or scary sounds.

A three-minute guide to the Goat's Great Day: meaning, character, love, work, money and lucky details. For people born in the Year of the Goat and zodiac fans on YouTube Shorts.

### Audio design
Music: Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. Keep it well below the voice (about 20 dB quieter) so every word is clear. Sound effects, used lightly: a soft chime when a lucky fact is revealed, a gentle whoosh on scene changes, a warm swell at the ending. No loud or scary sounds.

The pink lotus opens petal by petal in the moonlight, revealing an adorable golden goat with big soft eyes, curled shining horns and a tiny bell on a round golden pedestal. It opens its big sparkling eyes and its golden details begin to glow. Gentle close-up on its face, then pull back.

សម្រាប់លេខសំណាង៖ លេខប្រាំពីរ លេខម្ភៃប្រាំពីរ និងលេខពីររយកៅសិបពីរ។ សូមសរសេរលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នក នៅក្នុងមតិយោបល់ ហើយតោះនិយាយម្ដងទៀត៖ ប្រាំពីរ ម្ភៃប្រាំពីរ ពីររយកៅសិបពីរ!

## Long YouTube video – all 12 signs (about 5 minutes)
A long YouTube video (5:10): today's luckiest animal, an overview, then all 12 signs in order (two 10-second clips each), then a short closing. Lucky numbers are given only for today's lucky signs. Add the 12 character pictures as ingredients (each sign's clips use its own picture). Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Put the clips in order 1 to 31.

### YouTube chapters (paste into the description)
0:00 Introduction
0:40 Rat (ជូត) – Ordinary Day
1:00 Ox (ឆ្លូវ) – Take It Easy
1:20 Tiger (ខាល) – Ordinary Day
1:40 Rabbit (ថោះ) – Good Day
2:00 Dragon (រោង) – Ordinary Day
2:20 Snake (ម្សាញ់) – Ordinary Day
2:40 Horse (មមី) – Ordinary Day
3:00 Goat (មមែ) – Great Day
3:20 Monkey (វក) – Ordinary Day
3:40 Rooster (រកា) – Ordinary Day
4:00 Dog (ច) – Ordinary Day
4:20 Pig (កុរ) – Good Day
4:40 Closing

### Topic and audience
A full daily fortune for all twelve Chinese zodiac signs. For anyone who wants their own sign's rating, lucky color, best time and direction; chapters let each viewer jump to their sign.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Curiosity
   English: Three signs are lucky today, and one should take it easy. Where does your sign stand?
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ មានបីឆ្នាំដែលមានសំណាងល្អ និងមួយឆ្នាំដែលគួរសម្រាក។ តើឆ្នាំរបស់អ្នក នៅត្រង់ណា?
2. Question
   English: Which of the twelve zodiac signs is the luckiest today?
   Khmer: តើរាសីណា ក្នុងចំណោមរាសីទាំង 12 មានសំណាងជាងគេថ្ងៃនេះ?
3. Practical
   English: Your lucky color, best time, and direction for today, for all twelve signs, in about five minutes.
   Khmer: ពណ៌ ពេលវេលា និងទិសសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ សម្រាប់រាសីទាំង 12 ក្នុងរយៈពេលប្រហែលប្រាំនាទី។
4. Identity
   English: Whatever your sign, today has a lucky color and a best time just for you.
   Khmer: មិនថាអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំអ្វីទេ ថ្ងៃនេះមានពណ៌សំណាង និងពេលល្អ សម្រាប់អ្នក។
5. Daily fortune
   English: Today is a Goat day. Here's what that means for every sign.
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃមមែ។ មកមើលថា វាមានន័យយ៉ាងណា សម្រាប់រាសីនីមួយៗ។
★ = used to open this video. Why: Every viewer has a sign in this video, so this gives everyone a reason to keep watching until their chapter.

### Audio design
Music: Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. Keep it well below the voice (about 20 dB quieter) so every word is clear. Sound effects, used lightly: a soft chime when a lucky fact is revealed, a gentle whoosh on scene changes, a warm swell at the ending. No loud or scary sounds.

## Story video – YouTube (about 5 minutes)
"The Golden Goat and the Lost Lotus Seed" (ពពែមាស និងគ្រាប់ឈូកដែលបាត់): an original fairy tale (5:00) starring today's luckiest animal. A new story every day: today the golden goat and Phleu the firefly must find the lost seed before the full-moon festival. Today's lucky color, direction, best time, lucky numbers and lucky days are part of the story. Make the picture of Phleu once (prompt below) and add it and the Goat picture as ingredients in Flow. 30 clips of 10 seconds, in order 1 to 30.

### Character prompt – Phleu the firefly (make once, keep it)
Create a premium-quality 3D animated fantasy character of Phleu, a tiny cute glowing firefly with big sparkling eyes, a soft golden-green glow, delicate translucent wings and a tiny pink-and-gold flower crown, inspired by the charm of classic Disney and Pixar-style animated family films. Adorable expressive face, warm magical glow, on a glowing pink lotus by a pond at night, a softly lit Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat and a full moon behind. Cinematic lighting, beautiful depth of field. Square 1:1, full body visible, centered. No text, no letters, no watermark.

### YouTube chapters (paste into the description)
0:00 Once upon a time
1:20 The lucky color
1:40 The lucky path
2:30 The stone door
3:40 The full-moon festival
4:30 Today's luck

### Topic and audience
An original animated bedtime story starring the Goat, with today's lucky color, direction and numbers in the plot. For families, children and zodiac fans who enjoy gentle stories.

### Opening hook – 5 options

## Voice-over – story video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Can one little golden goat find the lost seed before the full moon rises? Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple in Cambodia, our story begins.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
But this morning, the lotus's magic seed was gone. Without it, the lotus could not bloom, and the festival could not begin.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
In the middle of the pond, a golden lotus opened, and out came a little golden goat. Today was a Goat day, and the goat felt something special.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
A firefly named Phleu flew down. "Little Goat," she said, "only you can find the lost seed. Today is your lucky day!"

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Gentle and kind, the Goat symbolizes peace, harmony, and a caring heart. Phleu smiled. That was exactly why the moon had chosen it.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
To bring back the lotus seed, the little hero needed three lucky treasures: its lucky color, its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
"Let's go!" said the goat, and it set off on its adventure, with Phleu right by its side.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
People born in the Year of the Goat are gentle, creative, and compassionate. Goats have a strong artistic sensibility and care deeply for others.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
The first treasure was waiting in a garden of a thousand lanterns. Only one shone brightest. It was green, the goat's lucky color today.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
The goat tied a green ribbon around its neck. "Wear your lucky color," said Phleu, "and good luck stays close to you."

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Next, they came to a crossroads with many paths. One path began to glow. It pointed to the east, the goat's lucky direction.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
But a wide river blocked the path, with only small stepping stones to cross it. The goat stopped. Sometimes, people born in the Year of the Goat can be indecisive or overly dependent on others.

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
Phleu came close and smiled. "Breathe slowly," she said. "Trust your heart. You are stronger than you think."

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
Stone by stone, the goat hopped across the river. Each stone glowed gold under its feet. On the other side, it laughed with joy.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
The little hero learned that its worries were small, and its good heart was big. Balance and courage always help us grow.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
At last, they reached a great stone door in the temple. Carved above it were bells, stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
"The door opens only at your best time," said Phleu. For the Goat today, that is from 5 to 7 in the morning. They waited patiently.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
When the time came, the goat rang the golden bells 7 times, its first lucky number. Ding! The door began to shine.

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
The door opened, and a stairway of light appeared. The goat climbed 27 glowing steps, its second lucky number, up to the top of the tower.

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
At the top of the tower, they found the magic lotus seed, glowing like a tiny sun. It was the heart of the giant lotus!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
Around the seed, seven little lights twinkled, one for each day of the week. Friday and Sunday shone brightest, the Goat's lucky days.

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
Time was running out. Holding the seed carefully, the little hero hurried home, with Phleu right beside it.

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting. The night was dark, and the full moon was rising. Was it too late?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
The goat placed the seed in the giant lotus. For a moment, nothing happened. Then, whoosh! The lotus opened in a burst of golden light!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
The giant lotus bloomed again. Lanterns rose into the sky, and the full-moon festival began. Everyone cheered for the little golden hero!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
The full moon shone down warmly, as if to say thank you. The goat felt peaceful and proud. It had done its very best.

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
If you were born in the Year of the Goat, today is your day too. Like our hero, be brave, be kind, and follow your good heart.

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: green. Best time: from 5 to 7 in the morning. Lucky direction: east. Lucky numbers: 7, 27, and 292.

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
And so, under the bright full moon, the little golden hero and Phleu fell fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

ប៉ុន្តែព្រឹកនេះ គ្រាប់វេទមន្តរបស់ផ្កាឈូក បានបាត់។ បើគ្មានវា ផ្កាឈូកមិនអាចរីកបាន ហើយពិធីបុណ្យក៏មិនអាចចាប់ផ្ដើមបានដែរ។

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
ពពែ ស្លូតបូត និងចិត្តល្អ។ ឆ្នាំមមែតំណាងឲ្យសន្តិភាព ភាពសុខដុម និងចិត្តមេត្តា។ ភ្លឺញញឹម។ នេះហើយ ជាហេតុដែលព្រះចន្ទ បានជ្រើសរើសវា។

ដើម្បីនាំគ្រាប់ឈូកត្រឡប់មកវិញ វីរបុរសតូច ត្រូវការរតនសម្បត្តិសំណាងបី៖ ពណ៌សំណាង ផ្លូវសំណាង និងលេខសំណាង។

ទ្វារបើក ហើយមានជណ្ដើរពន្លឺមួយ លេចឡើង។ ពពែឡើងជណ្ដើរភ្លឺ ចំនួនម្ភៃប្រាំពីរ កាំ ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីពីររបស់វា រហូតដល់កំពូលប្រាង្គ។

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
នៅលើកំពូលប្រាង្គ ពួកគេបានរកឃើញគ្រាប់ឈូកវេទមន្ត ភ្លឺដូចព្រះអាទិត្យតូចមួយ។ វាគឺជាបេះដូង នៃផ្កាឈូកដ៏ធំ!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
នៅជុំវិញគ្រាប់ឈូក មានពន្លឺតូចៗប្រាំពីរ ភ្លឺភ្លឹបភ្លែតៗ មួយសម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនីមួយៗក្នុងសប្ដាហ៍។ ថ្ងៃសុក្រ និងថ្ងៃអាទិត្យ ភ្លឺជាងគេ ដែលជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំមមែ។

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។ វីរបុរសតូច កាន់គ្រាប់ឈូកយ៉ាងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន ហើយប្រញាប់ត្រឡប់ទៅផ្ទះវិញ ដោយមានភ្លឺ នៅក្បែរ។

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។ យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។ តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
ពពែដាក់គ្រាប់ឈូក ចូលក្នុងផ្កាឈូកដ៏ធំ។ មួយសន្ទុះ គ្មានអ្វីកើតឡើងទេ។ បន្ទាប់មក វឺត! ផ្កាឈូករីក ដោយពន្លឺមាសចែងចាំង!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
ផ្កាឈូកដ៏ធំ រីកម្ដងទៀត។ គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។ ពពែមានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។ វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមែ ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។ ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)

```
ពួកគេរង់ចាំដោយអត់ធ្មត់។

69
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:52,746
ពេលដល់ម៉ោង ពពែវាយកណ្ដឹងមាស

70
00:02:52,796 --> 00:02:56,617
ចំនួន 7 ដង ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីមួយរបស់វា។

71
00:02:56,667 --> 00:02:57,907
ទីង!

72
00:02:57,957 --> 00:02:59,950
ទ្វារចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។

73
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:03,283
ទ្វារបើក ហើយមានជណ្ដើរពន្លឺមួយ លេចឡើង។

74
00:03:03,333 --> 00:03:05,896
ពពែឡើងជណ្ដើរភ្លឺ ចំនួន 27 កាំ

75
00:03:05,946 --> 00:03:09,950
ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីពីររបស់វា រហូតដល់កំពូលប្រាង្គ។

76
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:14,644
នៅលើកំពូលប្រាង្គ ពួកគេបានរកឃើញគ្រាប់ឈូកវេទមន្ត

77
00:03:14,694 --> 00:03:17,195
ភ្លឺដូចព្រះអាទិត្យតូចមួយ។

78
00:03:17,245 --> 00:03:19,950
វាគឺជាបេះដូង នៃផ្កាឈូកដ៏ធំ!

79
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:21,106
នៅជុំវិញគ្រាប់ឈូក

80
00:03:21,156 --> 00:03:23,555
មានពន្លឺតូចៗប្រាំពីរ ភ្លឺភ្លឹបភ្លែតៗ

81
00:03:23,605 --> 00:03:25,800
មួយសម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនីមួយៗក្នុងសប្ដាហ៍។

82
00:03:25,850 --> 00:03:28,113
ថ្ងៃសុក្រ និងថ្ងៃអាទិត្យ ភ្លឺជាងគេ

83
00:03:28,163 --> 00:03:29,950
ដែលជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំមមែ។

84
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:31,600
ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។

85
00:03:31,650 --> 00:03:35,581
វីរបុរសតូច កាន់គ្រាប់ឈូកយ៉ាងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន

86
00:03:35,631 --> 00:03:39,950
ហើយប្រញាប់ត្រឡប់ទៅផ្ទះវិញ ដោយមានភ្លឺ នៅក្បែរ។

87
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:44,485
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។

88
00:03:44,535 --> 00:03:48,438
យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។

89
00:03:48,488 --> 00:03:49,950
តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

90
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:53,344
ពពែដាក់គ្រាប់ឈូក ចូលក្នុងផ្កាឈូកដ៏ធំ។

91
00:03:53,394 --> 00:03:55,913
មួយសន្ទុះ គ្មានអ្វីកើតឡើងទេ។

92
00:03:55,963 --> 00:03:57,198
បន្ទាប់មក វឺត!

93
00:03:57,248 --> 00:03:59,950
ផ្កាឈូករីក ដោយពន្លឺមាសចែងចាំង!

94
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:01,968
ផ្កាឈូកដ៏ធំ រីកម្ដងទៀត។

95
00:04:02,018 --> 00:04:03,196
គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ

96
00:04:03,246 --> 00:04:06,617
ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។

97
00:04:06,667 --> 00:04:09,950
អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

98
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:13,147
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក

99
00:04:13,197 --> 00:04:15,032
ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។

100
00:04:15,082 --> 00:04:17,819
ពពែមានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។

101
00:04:17,869 --> 00:04:19,950
វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

102
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,916
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមែ ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។

103
00:04:23,966 --> 00:04:26,588
ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន

104
00:04:26,638 --> 00:04:29,950
មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

105
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,644
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។

106
00:04:31,694 --> 00:04:33,095
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌បៃតង។

107
00:04:33,145 --> 00:04:35,595
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 5 ដល់ម៉ោង 7 ព្រឹក។

108
00:04:35,645 --> 00:04:37,208
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងកើត។

109
00:04:37,258 --> 00:04:39,950
លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 7 លេខ 27 និងលេខ 292។

110
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:44,107
ហើយដូច្នេះ នៅក្រោមពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់

111
00:04:44,157 --> 00:04:47,478
វីរបុរសមាសតូច និងភ្លឺ ក៏លង់លក់

112
00:04:47,528 --> 00:04:49,950
ដោយសុបិនអំពីថ្ងៃស្អែក។

113
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:52,258
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

114
00:04:52,308 --> 00:04:54,373
អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំង 12 និងលេខសំណាង

115
00:04:54,423 --> 00:04:56,232
នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។

116
00:04:56,282 --> 00:04:58,540
សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។

117
00:04:58,590 --> 00:04:59,950
សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!
```

### Subtitles – English (2026-10-24-story.en.srt)
```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,728
Can one little golden goat find the lost

2
00:00:02,778 --> 00:00:04,950
seed before the full moon rises?

3
00:00:05,000 --> 00:00:07,867
Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple

4
00:00:07,917 --> 00:00:09,950
in Cambodia, our story begins.

5
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:11,306
But this morning

6
00:00:11,356 --> 00:00:14,018
the lotus's magic seed was gone.

7
00:00:14,068 --> 00:00:17,153
Without it, the lotus could not bloom

8
00:00:17,203 --> 00:00:19,950
and the festival could not begin.

9
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:22,900
In the middle of the pond, a golden lotus

10
00:00:22,950 --> 00:00:25,921
opened, and out came a little golden goat.

11
00:00:25,971 --> 00:00:27,360
Today was a Goat day

12
00:00:27,410 --> 00:00:29,950
and the goat felt something special.

13
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:32,782
A firefly named Phleu flew down.

14
00:00:32,832 --> 00:00:34,817
"Little Goat," she said

15
00:00:34,867 --> 00:00:37,738
"only you can find the lost seed.

16
00:00:37,788 --> 00:00:39,950
Today is your lucky day!"

17
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:42,785
Gentle and kind, the Goat symbolizes

18
00:00:42,835 --> 00:00:45,541
peace, harmony, and a caring heart.

19
00:00:45,591 --> 00:00:46,564
Phleu smiled.

20
00:00:46,614 --> 00:00:48,139
That was exactly why

21
00:00:48,189 --> 00:00:49,950
the moon had chosen it.

22
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,172
To bring back the lotus seed

23
00:00:52,222 --> 00:00:54,394
the little hero needed three

24
00:00:54,444 --> 00:00:56,934
lucky treasures: its lucky color

25
00:00:56,984 --> 00:00:59,950
its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

26
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:02,891
"Let's go!" said the goat

27
00:01:02,941 --> 00:01:06,538
and it set off on its adventure

28
00:01:06,588 --> 00:01:09,950
with Phleu right by its side.

29
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:12,415
People born in the Year of the Goat

30
00:01:12,465 --> 00:01:15,232
are gentle, creative, and compassionate.

31
00:01:15,282 --> 00:01:18,049
Goats have a strong artistic sensibility

32
00:01:18,099 --> 00:01:19,950
and care deeply for others.

33
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,223
The first treasure was waiting

34
00:01:22,273 --> 00:01:24,874
in a garden of a thousand lanterns.

35
00:01:24,924 --> 00:01:26,768
Only one shone brightest.

36
00:01:26,818 --> 00:01:28,586
It was green, the goat's

37
00:01:28,636 --> 00:01:29,950
lucky color today.

38
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:31,792
The goat tied a green

39
00:01:31,842 --> 00:01:33,810
ribbon around its neck.

40
00:01:33,860 --> 00:01:36,880
"Wear your lucky color," said Phleu

41
00:01:36,930 --> 00:01:39,950
"and good luck stays close to you."

42
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:42,555
Next, they came to a crossroads

43
00:01:42,605 --> 00:01:43,900
with many paths.

44
00:01:43,950 --> 00:01:45,832
One path began to glow.

45
00:01:45,882 --> 00:01:47,681
It pointed to the east

46
00:01:47,731 --> 00:01:49,950
the goat's lucky direction.

47
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:51,724
But a wide river blocked the path

48
00:01:51,774 --> 00:01:53,015
with only small stepping

49
00:01:53,065 --> 00:01:54,036
stones to cross it.

50
00:01:54,086 --> 00:01:54,950
The goat stopped.

51
00:01:55,000 --> 00:01:56,133
Sometimes, people born

52
00:01:56,183 --> 00:01:58,337
in the Year of the Goat can be indecisive

53
00:01:58,387 --> 00:01:59,950
or overly dependent on others.

54
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:02,592
Phleu came close and smiled.

55
00:02:02,642 --> 00:02:05,139
"Breathe slowly," she said.

56
00:02:05,189 --> 00:02:06,837
"Trust your heart.

57
00:02:06,887 --> 00:02:09,950
You are stronger than you think."

58
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:11,870
Stone by stone, the goat

59
00:02:11,920 --> 00:02:13,790
hopped across the river.

60
00:02:13,840 --> 00:02:16,830
Each stone glowed gold under its feet.

61
00:02:16,880 --> 00:02:19,950
On the other side, it laughed with joy.

62
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:23,311
The little hero learned that its worries

63
00:02:23,361 --> 00:02:26,589
were small, and its good heart was big.

64
00:02:26,639 --> 00:02:29,950
Balance and courage always help us grow.

65
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:32,347
At last, they reached a great

66
00:02:32,397 --> 00:02:34,413
stone door in the temple.

67
00:02:34,463 --> 00:02:36,562
Carved above it were bells

68
00:02:36,612 --> 00:02:39,950
stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

69
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,537
"The door opens only

70
00:02:41,587 --> 00:02:43,998
at your best time," said Phleu.

71
00:02:44,048 --> 00:02:45,426
For the Goat today

72
00:02:45,476 --> 00:02:48,204
that is from 5 to 7 in the morning.

73
00:02:48,254 --> 00:02:49,950
They waited patiently.

74
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:51,515
When the time came

75
00:02:51,565 --> 00:02:54,820
the goat rang the golden bells 7 times

76
00:02:54,870 --> 00:02:56,820
its first lucky number.

77
00:02:56,870 --> 00:02:57,863
Ding!

78
00:02:57,913 --> 00:02:59,950
The door began to shine.

79
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:01,095
The door opened

80
00:03:01,145 --> 00:03:03,614
and a stairway of light appeared.

81
00:03:03,664 --> 00:03:06,133
The goat climbed 27 glowing steps

82
00:03:06,183 --> 00:03:07,889
its second lucky number

83
00:03:07,939 --> 00:03:09,950
up to the top of the tower.

84
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:11,968
At the top of the tower

85
00:03:12,018 --> 00:03:14,687
they found the magic lotus seed

86
00:03:14,737 --> 00:03:16,792
glowing like a tiny sun.

87
00:03:16,842 --> 00:03:19,950
It was the heart of the giant lotus!

88
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:22,719
Around the seed, seven little lights

89
00:03:22,769 --> 00:03:25,719
twinkled, one for each day of the week.

90
00:03:25,769 --> 00:03:28,258
Friday and Sunday shone brightest

91
00:03:28,308 --> 00:03:29,950
the Goat's lucky days.

92
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:32,009
Time was running out.

93
00:03:32,059 --> 00:03:34,558
Holding the seed carefully

94
00:03:34,608 --> 00:03:37,303
the little hero hurried home

95
00:03:37,353 --> 00:03:39,950
with Phleu right beside it.

96
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:43,774
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting.

97
00:03:43,824 --> 00:03:45,538
The night was dark

98
00:03:45,588 --> 00:03:48,381
and the full moon was rising.

99
00:03:48,431 --> 00:03:49,950
Was it too late?

100
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:51,796
The goat placed the seed

101
00:03:51,846 --> 00:03:53,258
in the giant lotus.

102
00:03:53,308 --> 00:03:55,642
For a moment, nothing happened.

103
00:03:55,692 --> 00:03:56,642
Then, whoosh!

104
00:03:56,692 --> 00:03:57,873
The lotus opened

105
00:03:57,923 --> 00:03:59,950
in a burst of golden light!

106
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:02,223
The giant lotus bloomed again.

107
00:04:02,273 --> 00:04:04,192
Lanterns rose into the sky

108
00:04:04,242 --> 00:04:06,692
and the full-moon festival began.

109
00:04:06,742 --> 00:04:07,905
Everyone cheered

110
00:04:07,955 --> 00:04:09,950
for the little golden hero!

111
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:12,693
The full moon shone down warmly

112
00:04:12,743 --> 00:04:14,729
as if to say thank you.

113
00:04:14,779 --> 00:04:17,649
The goat felt peaceful and proud.

114
00:04:17,699 --> 00:04:19,950
It had done its very best.

115
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,256
If you were born in the Year of the Goat

116
00:04:23,306 --> 00:04:25,074
today is your day too.

117
00:04:25,124 --> 00:04:27,719
Like our hero, be brave, be kind

118
00:04:27,769 --> 00:04:29,950
and follow your good heart.

119
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,994
Let's remember today's luck.

120
00:04:32,044 --> 00:04:33,381
Lucky color: green.

121
00:04:33,431 --> 00:04:36,154
Best time: from 5 to 7 in the morning.

122
00:04:36,204 --> 00:04:37,760
Lucky direction: east.

123
00:04:37,810 --> 00:04:39,950
Lucky numbers: 7, 27, and 292.

124
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:43,188
And so, under the bright full moon

125
00:04:43,238 --> 00:04:46,712
the little golden hero and Phleu fell

126
00:04:46,762 --> 00:04:49,950
fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

127
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:51,617
That's your Chinese zodiac

128
00:04:51,667 --> 00:04:52,771
fortune for today.

129
00:04:52,821 --> 00:04:54,053
See all twelve signs

130
00:04:54,103 --> 00:04:56,745
and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign.com.

131
00:04:56,795 --> 00:04:58,860
Follow us for tomorrow's fortune.

132
00:04:58,910 --> 00:04:59,950
Have a lucky day!
```

## Khmer voice – how to get clear Khmer
Every Khmer voice line in this pack is written for speaking: short sentences, a ។ at each pause, and numbers written out in Khmer words (for example ហុកសិបបួន, not 64) so the voice does not read them in English. Subtitles and captions keep the digits.

### If you use Google Flow
Use "Copy + Khmer voice" for each scene; the voice description is already included. Flow's Khmer can be unclear on some clips. Listen to every clip, and remake any clip where words are wrong, cut off or English-sounding. If Khmer stays unclear, make the clips without voice ("Copy") and add a recorded Khmer voice-over instead.

### If you use a text-to-speech app
- Language: Cambodian Khmer (km-KH). Pick a real Khmer voice, for example Microsoft Azure's female voice "km-KH-SreymomNeural" (used by many TTS apps and sites). Do not use an English voice with Khmer text.
- Speed: 0.85× to 0.9×. Pitch: natural. Pause between sentences: normal.
- Paste the Khmer voice-over text from this pack exactly as it is (Khmer script, no romanization).
- Make one scene at a time if the app cuts off long text, then join the clips in your editor.
- Before using a new voice, test it with these two sentences and listen carefully:
  សួស្តីអ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា! សូមស្វាគមន៍មកកាន់ MyBirthSign.com។
  ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំខ្លា។ សូមជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង ហើយកុំភ្លេចអត់ធ្មត់។
  Check that every word is clear Cambodian Khmer, sentence endings are complete, and speed and volume stay even.

### Record it yourself
A native speaker reading the Khmer voice-over slowly and clearly is always the most natural choice.

## Scene 1 – Goat day
Magical and powerful. An adorable golden goat with big soft eyes, curled shining horns and a tiny bell stands on its glowing lotus pedestal on the pond in front of the Angkor-style temple under a huge full moon. Its eyes light up, a golden aura bursts around it, and it leaps from lotus to lotus across the pond, each landing bursting into golden light and blossoming flowers. The lotus pond ripples with golden light and lanterns rise into the sky. Dynamic camera: start close on its face, then sweep up and follow it through the air. Cute, majestic and full of energy, matching the reference image.

## Scene 2 – Lucky signs
Magical and powerful. An adorable golden goat with big soft eyes, curled shining horns and a tiny bell, an adorable golden rabbit with long shining ears, big sparkling eyes and a tiny pearl necklace and a chubby, adorable golden piglet with big happy eyes and rosy cheeks fly in from three directions on trails of golden light and land together on glowing lotus pedestals in front of the temple. As they land, a ring of golden energy explodes outward, lotus flowers bloom across the pond and a shower of sparkles and gold coins falls from the sky. They look at each other and cheer. The camera orbits around them in a smooth circle. The animals match the reference images.

## Scene 3 – Ox, take it easy
Premium 3D animated golden style, same characters as the reference pictures. A strong, adorable golden ox with big gentle eyes, polished curved horns and ruby studs on its harness curls up on a lotus pedestal as a soft protective bubble of golden light forms around it like a shield. Gentle glowing butterflies and petals circle it, and the full moon shines calmly on the still water. The mood is peaceful, safe and magical, not sad. The camera moves in slowly. The ox matches the reference image.

## Scene 4 – Outro
Magical and powerful. Golden light beams shoot up from the temple towers into the night sky and burst into gentle golden fireworks shaped like lotus flowers. Hundreds of floating lanterns rise over the glowing pond and the huge full moon. The camera rises up and pulls back for a wide, epic view, leaving open sky at the top of the frame for a title.

## Scene 5 – Lucky numbers (about 10–12 seconds)
Magical and powerful. A huge glowing golden zodiac wheel rises out of the lotus pond in front of the temple, with twelve cute golden zodiac animals around its rim. It spins faster and faster, shooting beams of golden light and sparkles, then slows to a gentle, shining turn under the full moon. The camera slowly pushes in, keeping open space in the middle and lower part of the frame for the number captions. (Loop the slow-turning part, or slow-zoom a still picture, to fill the time.)
